pan_dim_onium' timestamp='1309840978' post='4962575 wrote:
Tsk tsk. not quite Raise. Raise revives a character, but requires action after their death. What I'm looking for is the positive status ailment that automatically revives you after death without further action being needed by another character. Close though.
I don't want to seem like i'm arguing a point here, but i just wanted to note out that in some Final Fantasy games, such as Dirge of Cerberus and Crisis Core, it's just titled Raise, and it automatically revives you because you are working alone (it's activated by using a tuft/plume of phoenix down)

Ggaga bluesJul 16 2011, 10:44 AM
Q108: Jagds are lawless zones. These are Jagd Dorsa,Jagd Helje and Jagd Ahli. Since these zones are lawless (no Judge), the characters dont have to worry about getting a yellow or red cards for penalties, thats for the benefits. For the drawbacks, the character who is killed and failed to be revived after the battle will be considered dead for life (as in permanent). The players cant flee from battles in that zone as well.

Eexiled oneJul 23 2011, 04:48 AM
q116: If i recall correctly, ATB system was implemented chronologically first in FF4 even tho the ATB meter appeared first in FF5. The system was similar to the traditional turn-based system except it included the benefit of timing based strategy. It was basically like a gauge that would fill up over time (dependent i think on the speed of the character). Once full, the character would be able to attack, cast spells, use items ,etc. This would lead to the gauge depleting and the cycle repeats.q115: um going to go with black, green, fire, water, sky and earth. If i recall correctly Harle (the 7th dragon) was a combination of the other dragons.

SshadowmanwkpOct 05 2011, 09:37 AM
uwq1: Exclusive to the morpher class, from FFTA which is also a nu mou only class as well. The morpher class didn't reappear in FFTA2 regretfully >.> It is their default weapon type and the abilities they learn from them is transformation techniques. I said "default weapon" because they can morph into monsters and use various means of attacks by using them.Trivia: there's also the "orb of Minwu" accessory in FFTA and FFTA2 which has the description that it is also referred to as the "soul of Minwu" but is not a weapon, nor the item's real name. uwq2: Exclusive to Cait Sith only in FFVII, haven't played the game, but it is its trademark weapon which only it can equip.

monomer' timestamp='1318494661' post='4974157 wrote:
Blaster Edges are equipped by Rinoa in FF VIII Cloths are only used by Dancers in FF Tactics
Exclusive to the morpher class, from FFTA which is also a nu mou only class as well. The morpher class didn't reappear in FFTA2 regretfully >.> It is their default weapon type and the abilities they learn from them is transformation techniques. I said "default weapon" because they can morph into monsters and use various means of attacks by using them.Trivia: there's also the "orb of Minwu" accessory in FFTA and FFTA2 which has the description that it is also referred to as the "soul of Minwu" but is not a weapon, nor the item's real name

A lot of the remakes added Damage Limit Breaks, but Final Fantasy 8 was the first original to purposely allow it.

This quiz now covers Final Fantasy games with an extra question that covers any other SquareEnix game. It'll mostly cover the one's I've played and will also cover any games made by Square or Enix before their merger. Parasite Eve, Chrono Trigger, Dragon Quest, Einhander, Ehrgeiz, E.V.O., etc.

1. Obey the forum's posting rules. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.
2. One answer per person per question.
3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one as an Extra Question.
4. Questions are worth one point (unless otherwise specified). Extra questions are generally worth five, though that may vary.
5. Bonus questions are worth one point if offered and unless otherwise specified.
6. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.
7. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it. I may also accidentally repeat questions. It isn't intentional, but after so many pages, it is likely to happen.
8. This is based on the North American games or translated ROMs unless otherwise specified.
9. When answering the questions, you must specify which question you are answering by it's number. If you are answering an Extra Question, you must specify that it is EQ1, EQ2, etc.
10. If you had points when the quiz was run by a previous owner, feel free to let me know and tell me which page your points are on and I will gladly add them to my board.
11. If a question asks what the first game for XXX is, assume that I mean in order of North American release, unless otherwise specified.
